**Delivery rhythm:** implement a small useful batch, add focused unit tests for every changed code path, deliver it, and update this ledger before choosing the next batch through Root. Broad integration/system/performance checks happen after several delivered batches and at a final release checkpoint. They are not a separate feature roadmap, a repeated per-leaf gate or a reason to rerun unaffected tests. Observable acceptance below describes what players must be able to do; it does not prescribe an extensive test suite for each row.

All milestone statuses below are functional statuses. Prepared source/data, delivery location and qualified evidence are recorded separately. C/G/W/T/Q identifiers are stable ledger references to exact manifest IDs in the inventory below, not runtime aliases. Each mission has one primary milestone; prerequisite reuse never counts it twice.

## Status and delivery rules

Use exactly these four primary statuses for functional work:

| Status | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| completed | The named capability is usable with observable evidence on an exact identified deliverable. Record its build/source and limits. Authored assets, fixtures, unit passes or unmerged source alone never qualify. |
| pending integration | The bounded implementation has owner-focused unit tests PASS and a completed clean code review for bugs, performance and style, with exact frozen source/evidence. It awaits G1 composition. UNRUN/unreviewed packets do not qualify. |
| in development | Implementation or owner validation/review is underway, or usable acceptance is incomplete/blocked. A frozen patch with UNRUN tests/uncompleted review remains here, with the actual gap recorded. |
| not started | No implementation of this exact approved functional target is established. Designs/assets may be ready; shared engine code does not make its campaign playable. |

Delivery is a separate field: prepared branch; integrated source; verified local app; merged main; published release; published Homebrew. Record multiple locations when needed. Never infer progression between them. No L337 milestone is currently completed. Historical completed features may be reused, but predecessor completion is not current L337 acceptance. Unknown evidence is unknown, not an automatic pass or proof that every implementation is absent.

Neither is claimed playable. This is not a claim that shared engine work or authored TOML/art is missing. Prepared worker content outside this source snapshot is an explicit unknown U01.

A completed row requires its advertised prerequisites, exact stage-specific actions, legitimate outcomes and once-only settlement/clue. Decline/cancel/failure cannot award success; losses and current-format progress remain durable. Focused unit tests accompany each adaptation; broad combined playthroughs are batched after several content deliveries.

## Owned MBA pipeline

Superseding human policy: a useful frozen packet is not ready until owner-focused tests PASS and bugs/performance/style review is clean. Those gates are met for bounded MBA01/MBA04 integrated as e9c7b051 and final MBA05 native support integrated as1c129cca, as recorded below. Future native consumers and playable mission delivery remain open; no next feature is self-assigned.

**Owner:** designer maintains this queue; **Root alone dispatches through G3**, and G1 is sole integrator. Bounded MBA01/MBA04 and MBA05 implementation, owner-focused qualification/fixes and clean independent review are complete, with MBA05 exact final72d7167d integrated as1c129cca. MBA02 admission/first-success reconciliation is complete and folded into MBA03 because integrated generic admission already supplies the native prerequisite. Root now assigns G3 the bounded default G03 pack and atomic native first-success hook, with six focused owner units and independent review before integration. No Game/Director/generation/cash-framework expansion; production/usable mission status remains pending. Earlier packet/UNRUN rows are historical evidence, not current readiness.

The8GB/about60GB MBA handles lightweight isolated source/data authoring and bounded checks. Swift units may initially be authored UNRUN, but that packet stays in development. The owner must arrange focused execution under the approved lease, ensure PASS and complete bugs/performance/style review BEFORE integration handoff. G1 batches broad integration after several deliveries; unit responsibility is not deferred to an integration-ready handoff. No heavy game compiler, nativeGUI, full CI, profiling, new framework, direct canonical/main edit or current crash/fixture overlap. A pure model is not completed gameplay: every task names its actual consumer and native integration step.

### G03 authored quote and optional time rules

The approved `original-missions/original-families.md` Bonded freight passage section explicitly states **480 CR base +95 CR per full reachable route leg +80 CR per declared hazard tier**. With quoted full reachable leg count L and declared hazard tier H, reward is `480 + 95*L + 80*H` CR, or `4800 + 950*L + 800*H` native tenths. The75CR bond is separate: existing successful settlement returns the quoted reward plus750 native deposit once. The hazard premium is per declared tier, not always-on, per cargo tonne or per leg. Use checked arithmetic and existing cash/reward-plus-deposit bounds; no legacy schedule/multiplier.

The G03 JSON declares `deadlineSeconds=0` and `scanSeconds=0`: **the base family is untimed**. The shared authored contract requires preview of any explicit clock before Confirm, no unquoted deadline, pause-frozen armed flight simulation and current-format remainder preservation. A future timed variant must supply its positive finite duration through declared immutable quote data and current clock bounds; absent/0 is no deadline, never deadline-now or a difficulty/distance-generated legacy duration. Changing screens/loading cannot reroll the quoted terms or reset accepted time.

Genuine source ambiguities remain explicit: no hazard-tier taxonomy/route-government mapping, rule choosing shortest versus another valid quoted route, or positive optional duration is authored. For the current01 task, Root confirms selected full path.count-1 asL, immutable declaredH and optional timingdisabled. Keep other future route/hazard/duration variants validated data inputs with provenance; do not invent tuning or an undeclared hazard premium. `ENGINE-CONTRACT.md` specifies actual pickup as the start for Wayfarer budgets, while generic G03 says armed flight budget without a separate arming receipt. A future timed G03 variant must explicitly declare its native arming receipt; actual pickup (acceptance only if the same transaction) is the Wayfarer precedent, not a separately specified G03 rule. Leave that timed variant disabled until its receipt is declared. Base untimed G03 does not wait on a new timer policy. This documents the ambiguity rather than importing an old schedule.

## Maintenance and choosing the next work

1. Root consults M01-M19 before assigning the smallest player-useful batch. **At assignment**, immediately record milestone/item ID, owner/session, bounded files/scope, dependencies, primary status/readiness and next handoff in this ledger. All next work routes through Root; do not self-assign across workers.
2. **When the assigned delivery completes/freezes**, record its exact frozen commit/base/owned files, focused unit evidence or explicit UNRUN, blockers and prepared delivery location. Set pending integration only after the owner ensures focused unit tests PASS and a completed bugs/performance/style review is clean on the exact frozen source. UNRUN tests, Python-only checks and unreviewed packets remain in development/validation pending.
3. After integration, verification or release, update this ledger in the same handoff/commit before picking the next work. Record the actual candidate/main integration commit, exact verified build/release location, observable result, evidence location and remaining limitations. Keep functional status independent of prepared/integrated/local/main/released/Homebrew delivery.
4. The work-item owner implements the change, supplies focused unit tests and ensures they PASS. Obtain completed bugs/performance/style code review and close findings BEFORE integration handoff. Coordinate the existing execution lease; do not call UNRUN/unreviewed frozen source ready. Preserve relevant existing results. Group broad integration/system/performance validation after several delivered batches and at the final release checkpoint; do not repeatedly audit unaffected features, accumulate test counts as progress or turn refactors/docs/checks into independent player milestones.
5. Split a milestone into explicit subitems if a useful subset ships; do not hide unfinished content or mark the parent completed because some sources/tests pass. Every new/changed campaign/family/tutorial must retain its exact manifest ID, primary milestone and first-success clue mapping; reconcile counts when scope changes.
6. Preserve the historical delivery backlog, old evidence and frozen assets. Add newly discovered unknowns/blockers here rather than guessing. Update player README/help/equipment/mission/install instructions alongside the related delivered behavior; regenerate only media affected by actual source/asset changes, using the game renderer.
7. At the batched formal release checkpoint retain required completed clean PR review, protected final-head checks, current `make ci` coverage/sanitizer/leak gates and exact committed-main Sonar. Match signed/notarized assets/checksums/cask to the actual published source. A cancelled check or reviewer error never counts as approval.

This ledger itself is a documentation deliverable. Its creation runs no game/compiler/renderer/native/profiler/CI job and does not close a functional milestone. The paired [issue](ISSUE-completion-ledger.md) and [handoff](PR-completion-ledger.md) record its bounded validation and integration route.
